Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the calculus space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 135 and the convergence rate is O(393/n^5).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on calculus structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 135. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 393. QED.
